// @ref LLP 3000.011.003.000.000#2 — explicit saved bytes replace only source selection, never activation or store semantics.
use super::{Options, Project};
use std::path::Path;

/// Select one complete saved backend, or the ordinary multi-file project.
/// The vector is retained for both compilation and staging: one read, no swap.
pub(super) fn sources(project: &Project, options: &Options, flag: &str) -> Result<Vec<(String, String)>, String> {
    match options.flags.get(flag) {
        None => project.sources().map_err(|e| e.to_string()),
        Some(file) => {
            let path = Path::new(file);
            if !path.try_exists().map_err(|e|format!("cannot inspect saved source {file}: {e}"))? {return Err(format!("{file}: not saved yet: saved source does not exist"));}
            if path.is_dir() {
                return crate::client_compatibility::sources(path).map_err(|e| e.to_string());
            }
            let name = path.file_name().and_then(|s| s.to_str()).ok_or("saved source needs a UTF-8 file name")?;
            if !name.ends_with(".q") { return Err("saved source must be a complete Quarry .q file".into()); }
            let text = std::fs::read_to_string(path).map_err(|e| if e.kind()==std::io::ErrorKind::NotFound {format!("{file}: not saved yet: saved source does not exist")} else {format!("cannot read saved source {file}: {e}")})?;
            if text.trim().is_empty() { return Err(format!("{file}: not saved yet: saved Quarry source is empty")); }
            // A saved complete backend retains the schema namespace regardless of its archive file name.
            Ok(vec![("schema.q".into(), text)])
        }
    }
}

pub(super) fn compile(sources: &[(String, String)], options: &Options, flag: &str) -> snapback4_lang::Bound {
    let mut bound = snapback4_lang::compile(sources);
    if let Some(file) = options.flags.get(flag) {
        for diagnostic in &mut bound.diagnostics { diagnostic.site.file = if Path::new(file).is_dir() { Path::new(file).join(&diagnostic.site.file).to_string_lossy().into_owned() } else { file.clone() }; }
    }
    bound
}

/// A saved file can be used outside a project. Companion code is selected
/// only from an actual current project, never from arbitrary sibling files.
pub(super) fn project(options: &Options, flag: &str) -> Result<Project, String> {
    let cwd = std::env::current_dir().map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
    if let Some(project) = Project::locate(&cwd) { return Ok(project); }
    if options.flags.contains_key(flag) { return Ok(Project { root: cwd }); }
    Err("no snapback/*.q here or above; run `snapback4 init` or select a saved source file".into())
}

/// A private companion build uses the selected schema's generated contracts.
/// Packaging still records the original host installation, never this scratch.
pub(super) struct Candidate { pub project: Project, cleanup: std::path::PathBuf }
impl Drop for Candidate { fn drop(&mut self) { let _ = std::fs::remove_dir_all(&self.cleanup); } }
impl Candidate {
    pub(super) fn prepare(original: &Project, stage: &Path, bound: &snapback4_lang::Bound) -> Result<Self, String> {
        use std::sync::atomic::{AtomicUsize, Ordering};
        static SERIAL: AtomicUsize = AtomicUsize::new(0);
        let root = std::env::temp_dir().join(format!("snapback4-saved-source-{}-{}", std::process::id(), SERIAL.fetch_add(1, Ordering::Relaxed)));
        std::fs::create_dir(&root).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
        #[cfg(unix)] snapback4_core::private_fs::directory(&root).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
        let mut candidate = Self { project: Project { root: root.clone() }, cleanup: root };
        candidate.project.root = crate::dev_worker::private_scope(original, &candidate.cleanup)?;
        // The worker snapshotter captures the actual import closure and refuses
        // symlinks. Copy those captured bytes, not an unbounded application tree.
        copy_tree(stage, &candidate.project.root).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
        // Discover the current dependency graph without installing selected
        // contracts in the working project. A failed typecheck still publishes
        // its graph; the copied bytes are checked with selected contracts below.
        if let Some(entry) = crate::dev_worker::source(original) {
            let _ = original.check_entry(&entry);
            crate::dev_worker::package_entry(original, &candidate.project.root)?;
        }
        candidate.project.write_generated_from(bound, original).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
        if !crate::native::declared(bound).is_empty() && original.native_manifest().exists() {
            copy_tree(&original.native_dir(), &candidate.project.native_dir()).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
            rebase_tree(&candidate.project.native_dir(), &original.native_dir()).map_err(|e| format!("saved-source native snapshot: {e}"))?;
        }
        crate::dev_worker::link_dependencies(original, &candidate.project.root)?;
        if let Some(entry) = crate::dev_worker::source(&candidate.project) { candidate.project.check_entry(&entry)?; }
        Ok(candidate)
    }
}
fn copy_tree(source: &Path, target: &Path) -> std::io::Result<()> {
    std::fs::create_dir_all(target)?;
    for entry in std::fs::read_dir(source)? {
        let entry = entry?;
        if matches!(entry.file_name().to_str(), Some("target" | "native-target" | ".git")) { continue; }
        let kind = entry.file_type()?;
        if kind.is_dir() { copy_tree(&entry.path(), &target.join(entry.file_name()))?; }
        else if kind.is_file() { std::fs::copy(entry.path(), target.join(entry.file_name()))?; }
        else { return Err(std::io::Error::other("saved-source companion snapshot permits regular source files only")); }
    }
    Ok(())
}


#[cfg(test)]
//! The same fixture loader serves query testing and residency at either CLI entry point.
use super::Options;
use crate::project::Project;
use snapback4_core::{Engine, wire};
use snapback4_lang::Bound;

pub(super) fn engine(project: &Project, bound: &Bound, options: &Options) -> Result<Engine, String> {
    engine_at(project, bound, options, snapback4_core::now_ms())
}

pub(super) fn engine_at(project: &Project, bound: &Bound, options: &Options, now: i64) -> Result<Engine, String> {
    let Some(fixture) = options.flags.get("fixture") else {
        if options.flags.contains_key("source") {
            let store = snapback4_core::Store::memory().map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
            return Engine::new_at(store, bound.schema.clone(), bound.programs.iter().map(|p| p.program.clone()), now).map_err(|e| e.to_string());
        }
        return project.open_engine(bound).map_err(|error| format!("cannot open the store: {error}"));
    };
    let label=options.flags.get("fixture-input-label").unwrap_or(fixture);
    let started = std::time::Instant::now();
    let mut schema = bound.schema.clone();
    // Residency defers ordinary maintains until after bulk loading and builds
    // them once; column derives stay installed and maintain as rows insert.
    let residency = options.flags.contains_key("residency");
    if residency { schema.maintains.retain(|m| m.private.is_some()); }
    let store = snapback4_core::Store::memory().map_err(|error| format!("cannot open a store: {error}"))?;
    let mut engine = Engine::new_at(store, schema, bound.programs.iter().map(|p| p.program.clone()), now).map_err(|error| format!("cannot open a store: {error}"))?;
    let mut names = Vec::new();
    for entry in std::fs::read_dir(fixture).map_err(|error| format!("cannot read fixture directory {label}: {error}"))? {
        let entry = entry.map_err(|error| format!("cannot read fixture directory {label}: {error}"))?;
        names.push(entry.file_name().to_string_lossy().to_string());
    }
    names.sort();
    // One batch for the whole directory: the engine orders parents before
    // children within a batch, so file order (and read_dir order) cannot make
    // a read-through child or a reference miss its parent.
    let mut rows = Vec::new();
    let mut unknown = Vec::new();
    for name in names {
        let Some(table) = name.strip_suffix(".jsonl") else { continue };
        if name == super::test_cases::FILE { continue; }
        let path = std::path::Path::new(fixture).join(&name);
        let shown_path=std::path::Path::new(label).join(&name);
        let Some(definition) = bound.schema.tables.get(table) else {
            if options.flags.contains_key("saved-fixture-fallback") {
                eprintln!("test: skipped {} — saved source declares no table {table}; pass --fixture <dir> to select contemporary fixtures explicitly", shown_path.display());
                continue;
            }
            // @ref LLP 3000.011.003.000#7.4 — the starter's own unmodified sample is skipped loudly; a project's file for a missing table still refuses.
            if !options.flags.contains_key("source") && super::init::starter_fixture(&name).is_some_and(|starter| std::fs::read_to_string(&path).is_ok_and(|text| text == starter)) {
                eprintln!("test: WARNING: skipped {} — the todo starter's unmodified sample rows for a `{table}` table your snapback/*.q does not declare; `snapback4 init --adapt` removes it and the starter's other leftovers", shown_path.display());
                continue;
            }
            unknown.push(name);
            continue;
        };
        let text = std::fs::read_to_string(&path).map_err(|error| format!("cannot read fixture file {}: {error}", shown_path.display()))?;
        for (line_number, line) in text.lines().enumerate().filter(|(_, line)| !line.trim().is_empty()) {
            let at = format!("{name}:{}", line_number + 1);
            let mut json: serde_json::Value = serde_json::from_str(line).map_err(|error| format!("{at}: {error}"))?;
            // A residency fixture is often an exported store: engine-maintained
            // columns are dropped and rebuilt from the source rows. `test`
            // refuses them as an insert and `/seed` do (E_MAINTAINED_COLUMN).
            if residency && let Some(fields) = json.as_object_mut() { fields.retain(|field, _| !definition.maintained.contains_key(field)); }
            relative_times(definition, &mut json, now).map_err(|why| format!("{at}: {why}"))?;
            if let Some(why) = wire::bare_principal(definition, &json) { return Err(format!("{at}: {why}")); }
            let row = wire::row_from_json(definition, &json).map_err(|error| format!("{at}: {error}\n  hint: a fixture row is one JSON object per line with the table's fields and an \"id\"; see snapback4 guide testing"))?;
            rows.push((table.to_owned(), row));
        }
    }
    // A misspelled file name would otherwise load nothing and still pass.
    if !unknown.is_empty() {
        let tables: Vec<&str> = bound.schema.visible_tables().map(|(name, _)| name.as_str()).collect();
        // 0.4.17's init wrote the todo starter's rows beside a project's own schema.
        let starter = unknown.iter().any(|name| name == "todos.jsonl")
            && std::fs::read_to_string(std::path::Path::new(fixture).join("todos.jsonl")).is_ok_and(|text| text.contains("\"id\":\"todo-milk\""));
        let starter = if starter { format!("\n  hint: todos.jsonl is the todo starter's sample that `snapback4 init` wrote; `snapback4 init --adapt` removes it and the starter's {} while they are unmodified (or delete them, and the starter's list/add/toggle/remove lines, yourself)", super::test_cases::FILE) } else { String::new() };
        return Err(format!("{label}: {} name{} no table in snapback/*.q; nothing was run{starter}\n  hint: a fixture file is <table>.jsonl for a declared table ({}), plus {}; see snapback4 guide testing",
            unknown.join(", "), if unknown.len() == 1 { "s" } else { "" }, tables.join(", "), super::test_cases::FILE));
    }
    // Fixture rows take `/seed`'s insert path (defaults, sequences, unique with the author's codes, caps, maintained views), not the trusted bulk import.
    let loaded = engine.seed_at(rows, now).map_err(|error| {
        let text = error.to_string();
        let hint = if text.contains("NOT_FOUND") { "\n  hint: every fixture file loads as one batch, parents first; NOT_FOUND means a referenced or read-through parent row is in no fixture file (check the id)" } else { "" };
        format!("{label}: {text}{hint}")
    })?;
    if residency {
        engine.adopt_at(bound.schema.clone(), bound.programs.iter().map(|p| p.program.clone()), now).map_err(|error| format!("cannot build fixture maintains: {error}"))?;
    }
    if !options.json { println!("test: loaded {loaded} rows from {label} in {:.1} s", started.elapsed().as_secs_f64()); }

//! Wave-19 security regressions: trust belongs to authored fixtures, not proposals.
use snapback4_lang::{compile,security::{self,challenges::{self,Campaign,Reachability}}};
use snapback4_core::{Engine,Value};
fn bind(source:&str)->snapback4_lang::Bound{let b=compile(&[("schema.q".into(),source.into())]);assert!(b.diagnostics.is_empty(),"{:?}",b.diagnostics);b}
const ROLES:&str="use identity password\ntable roles:\n  principal: principal\n  role: enum('doctor','volunteer')\n  unique principal\n  read <- allow\n  insert <- viewer = 'service:operator'\n  update <- viewer = 'service:operator'\n  online only\n";
fn row(fields:Vec<(&str,Value)>)->Value{Value::Row(fields.into_iter().map(|(k,v)|(k.into(),v)).collect())}
fn id(v:&str)->Value{Value::Id(v.into())}
#[test]
fn authored_seed_read_and_integrity_refutations_fail_without_an_initialization_history(){
 let b=bind("table docs:\n  owner: principal\n  approved: bool\n  read <- allow\n  online only\ninvariant docs.read only .owner = viewer\ninvariant docs always not .approved\n");
 let mut e=Engine::memory(b.schema.clone(),b.programs.iter().map(|p|p.program.clone())).unwrap();
 e.seed_at([("docs".into(),row(vec![("id",id("doc")),("owner",id("dev:owner")),("approved",Value::Bool(true))]))],challenges::NOW).unwrap();
 let mut c=Campaign::default();challenges::fixture_observers(&b,&e,&mut c);
 for inv in &b.invariants{assert!(c.violations.iter().any(|v|v.invariant_site==inv.site.to_string()&&v.reachability.is_reachable()),"{} {c:?}",inv.target);}
 let mut r=security::analyze(&b);challenges::apply(&mut r,&c);assert!(r.refuted());
 let synthetic=challenges::run(&b);assert!(synthetic.violations.iter().all(|v|!v.reachability.is_reachable()));
}
#[test]
fn generated_payloads_preserve_creator_copies_and_cold_approval_requirements(){
 let source=format!("{ROLES}table homes:\n  owner: principal\n  read <- allow\n  insert <- allow\n  online only\ntable reports:\n  home: homes\n  homeowner: principal\n  coldApproved: bool\n  reason: text <=40 ?\n  read <- allow\n  insert <- allow\n  online only\nmutation file(home: homes):\n  h = homes[home] ! else MISSING\n  return insert reports {{home,homeowner: h.owner,coldApproved: false,reason: null}}\ninvariant reports always not .coldApproved or .reason != null\n");
 let b=bind(&source);let mut c=Campaign::default();let e=challenges::generated_fixture(&b,&mut c).unwrap();
 let homes=e.store().rows("homes").unwrap();let reports=e.store().rows("reports").unwrap();assert!(!reports.is_empty(),"{c:?}");
 for r in reports{let home=homes.iter().find(|h|h.field("id")==r.field("home")).unwrap();assert_eq!(r.field("homeowner"),home.field("owner"));assert_eq!(r.field("coldApproved").unwrap(),&Value::Bool(false));}
 challenges::fixture_observers(&b,&e,&mut c);assert!(c.violations.is_empty(),"{c:?}");
}
#[test]
fn generated_assignments_obey_authored_recipient_role_guards(){
 let source=format!("{ROLES}table assignments:\n  assignee: principal\n  read <- .assignee = viewer\n  insert <- exists roles[viewer,role = 'doctor']\n  online only\nmutation assign(who: principal):\n  require exists roles[principal = who,role = 'doctor'] else WRONG_ROLE\n  return insert assignments {{assignee: who}}\ninvariant assignments.read only exists roles[viewer,role = 'doctor']\nquery mine():\n  return assignments first 50 by .id\n");
 let b=bind(&source);let mut c=Campaign::default();let e=challenges::generated_fixture(&b,&mut c).unwrap();let rs=e.store().rows("assignments").unwrap();assert!(!rs.is_empty(),"{c:?}");
 assert!(rs.iter().all(|r|r.field("assignee").unwrap().as_str().unwrap().contains("doctor")));
 challenges::fixture_observers(&b,&e,&mut c);assert!(c.violations.is_empty(),"{c:?}");
}
#[test]
fn generated_rows_cannot_bypass_an_impossible_insert_gate(){

 let mut c=Campaign::default();let e=challenges::generated_fixture(&b,&mut c).unwrap();assert!(e.store().rows("secrets").unwrap().is_empty());challenges::fixture_observers(&b,&e,&mut c);assert!(c.violations.is_empty());assert!(c.unsupported.iter().any(|s|s.contains("no admitted insert")));
}

fn negative_existence_column_intent_only_refutes_the_excluded_role(){

 let mut c=Campaign::default();c.generated_fixture=true;let e=challenges::generated_fixture(&b,&mut c).unwrap();challenges::fixture_observers(&b,&e,&mut c);
 assert!(c.violations.iter().any(|v|v.viewer.contains("volunteer")),"{c:?}");assert!(c.violations.iter().all(|v|v.viewer.contains("volunteer")),"{c:?}");
}

#[test]

}

#[test]

 let mut e=Engine::memory(b.schema.clone(),b.programs.iter().map(|p|p.program.clone())).unwrap();
 e.seed_at([("roles".into(),row(vec![("id",id("role")),("principal",id("dev:worker")),("role",Value::String("doctor".into()))])),("jobs".into(),row(vec![("id",id("job")),("worker",id("dev:worker")),("body",Value::String("private".into()))]))],challenges::NOW).unwrap();
 let mut c=Campaign::default();challenges::fixture_observers(&b,&e,&mut c);
 assert!(c.violations.iter().any(|v|v.reachability==Reachability::AuthoredFixtureTrace&&v.operation=="jobs"&&v.history.iter().any(|s|s.operation=="provision"&&s.args.get("role")==Some(&Value::String("volunteer".into()))&&s.result["state"]=="sent")),"{c:?}");
}

}

#[test]
fn a_post_insert_guard_through_the_returned_row_preserves_owner_coupling(){
 let b=bind("table homes:\n  owner: principal\n  read <- allow\n  insert <- allow\n  online only\ntable reports:\n  home: homes\n  homeowner: principal\n  immutable home,homeowner\n  read <- .homeowner = viewer\n  insert <- allow\n  online only\nmutation file(home: homes, homeowner: principal):\n  r = insert reports {home,homeowner}\n  h = homes[home] ! else MISSING\n  require r.homeowner = h.owner else WRONG_OWNER\n  return r\nquery mine():\n  return reports where .homeowner = viewer first 50 by .id\ninvariant reports.homeowner read only .home.owner = viewer\n");
 let mut e=Engine::memory(b.schema.clone(),b.programs.iter().map(|p|p.program.clone())).unwrap();
 e.seed_at([("homes".into(),row(vec![("id",id("h")),("owner",id("dev:owner"))])),("reports".into(),row(vec![("id",id("r")),("home",id("h")),("homeowner",id("dev:owner"))]))],challenges::NOW).unwrap();
 let args=std::collections::BTreeMap::from([("home".into(),id("h")),("homeowner".into(),id("dev:security-a"))]);
 let rejected=e.mutate("file",None,snapback4_core::Context::new("dev:security-a",args).at(challenges::NOW)).unwrap();
 assert!(matches!(rejected.state,snapback4_core::WriteState::Failed{..}));
 let observed=e.query("mine",snapback4_core::Context::new("dev:security-a",std::collections::BTreeMap::new()).at(challenges::NOW)).unwrap();assert_eq!(observed.data,Value::Array(Vec::new()));
 let mut c=Campaign::default();challenges::fixture_observers(&b,&e,&mut c);
 assert!(c.violations.is_empty(),"{c:?}");assert!(c.unsupported.iter().any(|s|s.contains("creator/guard coupling")),"{c:?}");
}

}

#[test]
fn ephemeral_mutations_are_uncovered_in_every_column_observer_phase(){
 let source="table docs:\n  body: int\n  read <- allow\n  insert <- allow\n  online only\ntable pulses:\n  principal: principal\n  unique principal\n  read <- allow\n  insert <- .principal = viewer\n  update <- .principal = viewer\n  ephemeral 4s\nmutation ping():\n  upsert pulses[viewer] {}\n  return docs first 10 by .id {body}\ninvariant docs.body read only deny\n";
 let b=bind(source);let mut c=Campaign::default();challenges::generated_campaign(&b,&mut c,true);
 assert!(c.violations.is_empty(),"{c:?}");assert!(!c.operations.contains_key("ping"),"{c:?}");assert!(c.unsupported.iter().any(|s|s.contains("ping")&&s.contains("ephemeral")),"{c:?}");
 let b=bind(&format!("{source}query observe():\n  return docs first 10 by .id {{body}}\n"));let mut c=Campaign::default();challenges::generated_campaign(&b,&mut c,true);
 assert!(c.violations.iter().any(|v|v.operation=="observe"&&v.reachability==Reachability::GeneratedFixtureTrace),"{c:?}");
 assert!(!c.operations.contains_key("ping"),"{c:?}");
}

    fn dependency(value:&serde_json::Value,arg:&str,table:&str,args:&BTreeMap<String,ir::ArgType>,bindings:&BTreeMap<String,serde_json::Value>,depth:usize)->bool{
        if depth>32{return true;}
        if let Some(field)=value.get("Field")&&let (Some(base),Some(name))=(field.get("base"),field.get("name").and_then(serde_json::Value::as_str))&&let Some(map)=fields(base,bindings,depth+1){
            return map.get(name).is_none_or(|expression|dependency(expression,arg,table,args,bindings,depth+1));
        }
        if let Some(name)=value.get("Variable").and_then(serde_json::Value::as_str){
            if name==arg{return true;}
            if let Some(bound)=bindings.get(name){return bound.is_null()||dependency(bound,arg,table,args,bindings,depth+1);}
            return !args.contains_key(name);
        }
        // A guard re-reading the written table may inspect the supplied field
        // through a row alias even when the argument itself occurs only once.
        for tag in ["Get","Exists"]{if value.get(tag).and_then(|v|v.get("table")).and_then(serde_json::Value::as_str)==Some(table){return true;}}
        match value{serde_json::Value::Object(xs)=>xs.values().any(|v|dependency(v,arg,table,args,bindings,depth+1)),serde_json::Value::Array(xs)=>xs.iter().any(|v|dependency(v,arg,table,args,bindings,depth+1)),_=>false}
    }
    fn guarded(value:&serde_json::Value,arg:&str,table:&str,args:&BTreeMap<String,ir::ArgType>,bindings:&BTreeMap<String,serde_json::Value>)->bool{
        for tag in ["Require","Check","If"]{if let Some(condition)=value.get(tag).and_then(|v|v.get("condition"))&&dependency(condition,arg,table,args,bindings,0){return true;}}
        for tag in ["Binary","Unary","Builtin","Emit","JobAs","JobQuery"]{if let Some(expression)=value.get(tag){
            if tag=="Builtin"&&expression.get("builtin").is_some_and(|b|b.get("ProjectFields").is_some()){continue;}
            if dependency(expression,arg,table,args,bindings,0){return true;}
        }}
        match value{serde_json::Value::Object(xs)=>xs.values().any(|v|guarded(v,arg,table,args,bindings)),serde_json::Value::Array(xs)=>xs.iter().any(|v|guarded(v,arg,table,args,bindings)),_=>false}
    }
    fn related_write(value:&serde_json::Value,arg:&str,table:&str,column:&str,args:&BTreeMap<String,ir::ArgType>,bindings:&BTreeMap<String,serde_json::Value>)->bool{
        for tag in ["Insert","InsertIfAbsent","Upsert","Update","WriteTarget"]{
            let Some(write)=value.get(tag)else{continue;};
            let name=write.get("table").or_else(||write.get("target").and_then(|t|t.get("table"))).and_then(serde_json::Value::as_str);
            if let Some(row)=write.get("row").or_else(||write.get("patch")){
                if let Some(map)=fields(row,bindings,0){
                    if map.iter().any(|(field,expression)|(name!=Some(table)||field!=column)&&dependency(expression,arg,table,args,bindings,0)){return true;}
                }else if dependency(row,arg,table,args,bindings,0){return true;}
            }
            if write.get("key").or_else(||write.get("target").and_then(|target|target.get("key"))).is_some_and(|key|dependency(key,arg,table,args,bindings,0))||write.get("condition").is_some_and(|condition|dependency(condition,arg,table,args,bindings,0)){return true;}
        }
        match value{serde_json::Value::Object(xs)=>xs.values().any(|v|related_write(v,arg,table,column,args,bindings)),serde_json::Value::Array(xs)=>xs.iter().any(|v|related_write(v,arg,table,column,args,bindings)),_=>false}
    }
    fn writes(value:&serde_json::Value,table:&str,column:&str,args:&BTreeMap<String,ir::ArgType>,program:&str,bindings:&BTreeMap<String,serde_json::Value>,guards:&BTreeSet<String>,defaulted:bool,found:&mut bool,coupled:&mut bool){
        if let Some(object)=value.as_object(){
            for tag in ["Insert","InsertIfAbsent","Upsert","Update","WriteTarget"]{
                let Some(write)=object.get(tag)else{continue;};
                let name=write.get("table").or_else(||write.get("target").and_then(|t|t.get("table"))).and_then(serde_json::Value::as_str);
                if name!=Some(table){continue;}
                let map=write.get("row").or_else(||write.get("patch")).and_then(|e|fields(e,bindings,0));
                let Some(map)=map else{*found=true;*coupled=true;continue;};
                if let Some(expression)=map.get(column){
                    *found=true;
                    if let Some(name)=expression.get("Variable").and_then(serde_json::Value::as_str)&&args.contains_key(name){
                        let occurrence=serde_json::to_string(&Expr::Variable(name.into())).unwrap();
                        if program.matches(&occurrence).count()!=1||guards.contains(name){*coupled=true;}
                    }else{*coupled=true;}
                }else if tag=="Upsert"||defaulted&&matches!(tag,"Insert"|"InsertIfAbsent"){*found=true;*coupled=true;}
            }
            for child in object.values(){writes(child,table,column,args,program,bindings,guards,defaulted,found,coupled);}
        }else if let Some(xs)=value.as_array(){for child in xs{writes(child,table,column,args,program,bindings,guards,defaulted,found,coupled);}}
    }
    let(mut found,mut unsafe_writer)=(false,false);
    for p in &bound.programs{let json=serde_json::to_value(&p.program).unwrap();let text=serde_json::to_string(&p.program).unwrap();let mut bindings=BTreeMap::new();definitions(&json,&mut bindings);let guards=p.program.args.keys().filter(|arg|guarded(&json,arg,&inv.table,&p.program.args,&bindings)||related_write(&json,arg,&inv.table,column,&p.program.args,&bindings)).cloned().collect();writes(&json,&inv.table,column,&p.program.args,&text,&bindings,&guards,table.defaults.contains_key(column),&mut found,&mut unsafe_writer);}
    found&&unsafe_writer
}
fn run_pair_variant(bound:&Bound,inv:&Invariant,rows:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,row:&Value,fixture:Option<&Engine>,trace:Option<(&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,&[histories::Step])>,presence:bool,out:&mut Campaign){
    if discharged(bound,inv){return;}
    if coupled(bound,inv){out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: alternate value may break authored creator/guard coupling; only actual typed payloads and committed histories count",inv.target_label()));return;}
    if !reserve(out,1){return;}
    let column=inv.column.as_deref().unwrap();
    let kind=&bound.schema.tables[&inv.table].columns[column];
    let mut other=match sample_rows(bound,rows,inv,row,presence){
        Some(other)=>other,
        None if kind.contains_asset()&&fixture.is_some()=>{
            let mut other=rows.clone();
            let Some(value)=sample(kind.required(),4)else{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}.{}: alternate media type unsupported",inv.table,column));return;};
            if let Some(Value::Row(fields))=other.get_mut(&inv.table).and_then(|rs|rs.iter_mut().find(|r|r.field("id").ok()==row.field("id").ok())){fields.insert(column.into(),value);}other
        },
        None=>{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}.{}: column pair needs a second typed value and, for media, a coupled verified fixture ledger",inv.table,column));return;},
    };
    // Both worlds receive the same verified ledger, including the alternate
    // upload. Only the protected row value changes; no fabricated media ids.
    let ledger;
    let fixture=if kind.contains_asset(){
        let Some(base)=fixture else{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}.{}: media column pair requires the fixture ledger",inv.table,column));return;};
        let mut prepared=match fork(bound,base,Vec::new()){Ok(e)=>e,Err(why)=>{out.unsupported.insert(why);return;}};
        let target=other.get_mut(&inv.table).unwrap().iter_mut().find(|r|r.field("id").ok()==row.field("id").ok()).unwrap();
        if let Value::Row(fields)=target {
            let value=match hydrate(&mut prepared,kind,&fields[column]){Ok(v)=>v,Err(why)=>{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}.{}: alternate media upload: {why}",inv.table,column));return;}};
            fields.insert(column.into(),value);
        }
        ledger=prepared;Some(&ledger)
    }else{fixture};
    let altrow=other[&inv.table].iter().find(|r|r.field("id").ok()==row.field("id").ok()).unwrap();
    let observing=histories::observer_tables(bound,&inv.table);
    let mut actors=histories::viewers(bound);
    for (table,rs) in rows{for r in rs{for (c,k) in &bound.schema.tables[table].columns{if matches!(k.required(),C::Principal)&&let Ok(Value::Id(p))=r.field(c)&&!actors.contains(p){actors.push(p.clone());}}}}
    'actors: for viewer in &actors{
        if fixture.is_some()&&out.violations.iter().any(|v|v.invariant_site==inv.site.to_string()&&v.viewer==*viewer&&v.reachability==Reachability::AuthoredFixtureTrace&&histories::trace_key(&v.history)==histories::trace_key(trace.map(|(_,s)|s).unwrap_or(&[]))){continue;}

        {
            if !reserve(out,2){return;}
            let judge=if let Some(fixture)=fixture{fork(bound,fixture,Vec::new())}else{engine(bound,rows,program("$column.intent",ProgramKind::Query,vec![Stmt::Return(Expr::Literal(Value::Null))]))};
            let Ok(mut judge)=judge else{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: primary column intent world unavailable",inv.target_label()));continue;};
            let actual=match readback(&judge){Ok(rows)=>rows,Err(why)=>{out.unsupported.insert(why);continue;}};
            let Some(primary)=actual.get(&inv.table).into_iter().flatten().find(|r|r.field("id").ok()==row.field("id").ok())else{continue;};
            if policy_in(bound,inv,&actual,viewer,None,primary,&mut judge)!=Ok(false){continue;}
        }
        for bp in &bound.programs{
            if !reserve(out,1){return;}
            let p=&bp.program;
            if ephemeral_operation(bound,p,out){continue;}
            let json=serde_json::to_value(p).unwrap();
            if !observing.iter().any(|table|super::super::audit::mentions(&json,table)){continue;}
            if !matches!(p.kind,ProgramKind::Query|ProgramKind::Mutation)||p.native{continue;}
            let json=serde_json::to_string(p).unwrap();
            if ["NewId","Emit","JobAs","JobQuery"].iter().any(|s|json.contains(s)){out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: generated-id/opaque column observer declined",p.name));continue;}
            if p.kind==ProgramKind::Mutation {
                if inv.policy.is_none(){out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: column mutation has unknown policy dependencies; disclosure-boundary tracing required",p.name));continue;}
                let writes=ir::write_tables(p);
                let deps=inv.policy.as_ref().map(|r|stability::dependencies(bound,r)).unwrap_or_default();
                if writes.iter().any(|(t,_)|t==&inv.table||deps.contains(t)){
                    out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: column mutation can change protected row/policy before disclosure; boundary tracing required",p.name));continue;
                }
                if stability::implicit(bound,p).iter().any(|t|t==&inv.table||deps.contains(t)){out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: implicit writes can change column policy before disclosure",p.name));continue;}
            }
            let args_list=histories::fixture_cases(p,rows,&actors,out);
            for args in args_list{
                if !reserve(out,12){return;}
                let execute=|alternate:bool|->Result<(serde_json::Value,BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,BTreeMap<String,Value>),String>{
                    let seeds=if alternate{&other}else{rows};
                    let mut e=if let Some(fixture)=fixture{
                        let mut e=fork(bound,fixture,Vec::new())?;
                        if alternate{
                            let t=&bound.schema.tables[&inv.table];
                            let Value::Row(fields)=altrow else{return Err("not a row".into());};
                            let clean=Value::Row(fields.iter().filter(|(c,_)|!t.engine_field(c)&&!t.recorded.contains_key(*c)&&!t.maintained.contains_key(*c)).map(|(c,v)|(c.clone(),v.clone())).collect());
                            if let Err(why)=e.seed_at([(inv.table.clone(),clean.clone())],NOW){
                                let immutable=t.constraints.iter().any(|c|matches!(c,Constraint::Immutable{fields} if inv.column.as_ref().is_some_and(|c|fields.contains(c)))||matches!(c,Constraint::AppendOnly))||matches!(t.columns[column].required(),C::Image{once:true,..});
                                if !immutable{return Err(why.to_string());}
                                if fixture.ephemeral_hub().snapshot().map_err(|e|e.to_string())?.version.epoch!=0{return Err("immutable column alternate would drop existing ephemeral state; reduction uncovered".into());}
                                e=engine_on_store(bound,&other,Vec::new(),fixture.store().empty_with_asset_ledger().map_err(|e|e.to_string())?)?;
                            }
                        }e
                    }else{engine(bound,seeds,program("$column.pair",ProgramKind::Query,vec![Stmt::Return(Expr::Literal(Value::Null))]))?};
                    validate_fixture(bound,&mut e)?;
                    let actual=readback(&e)?;
                    let current=actual.get(&inv.table).into_iter().flatten().find(|r|r.field("id").ok()==row.field("id").ok()).ok_or("column target is absent after validation")?;
                    if policy_in(bound,inv,&actual,viewer,None,current,&mut e)!=Ok(false){return Err("validated column-world policy is not false".into());}
                    let actual_args=materialize_arguments(&mut e,&p.args,&args,viewer)?;
                    let observed=paired::observe(&mut e,p,viewer,actual_args.clone())?;
                    Ok((observed,actual,actual_args))
                };
                let (a,primary_rows,primary_args,b,alternate_rows,alternate_args)=match (execute(false),execute(true)){
                    (Ok((a,primary,aa)),Ok((b,alternate,ba)))=>(a,primary,aa,b,alternate,ba),
                    (Err(why),_)|(_,Err(why))=>{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}.{} / {}: declined column world: {why}",inv.table,inv.column.as_deref().unwrap_or(""),p.name));continue;}
                };
                out.attempted+=2;*out.operations.entry(p.name.clone()).or_default()+=2;
                if primary_args!=alternate_args{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: paired column arguments require a coupled asset ledger; differing materialized inputs declined",p.name));continue;}
                let Ok(key)=row.field("id")else{continue;};
                if !same_base_world(bound,inv,&primary_rows,&alternate_rows,key){out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}.{}: committed worlds change non-protected base fields/captures or populations; reduction unavailable",inv.table,inv.column.as_deref().unwrap()));continue;}
                if a==b{continue;}

    use crate::types::Ty;
    match ty{Ty::Row(t)|Ty::WriteRow(t)|Ty::Page(t,_)=>t==table,Ty::Optional(inner)|Ty::List(inner,_)|Ty::Collection(inner,_)=>typed_source(inner,table),Ty::Object(fields)|Ty::RowShape(fields)|Ty::RowObject(fields)=>fields.values().any(|ty|typed_source(ty,table)),_=>false}

pub(super) fn observer_tables(bound:&Bound,table:&str)->BTreeSet<String>{
    let mut out=BTreeSet::from([table.into()]);
    loop{let before=out.len();
        for m in &bound.schema.maintains{if out.contains(&m.source){out.insert(m.target.clone());if let Some(owner)=&m.private{out.insert(owner.table.clone());}}}
        for (name,t) in &bound.schema.tables{if t.through.as_ref().is_some_and(|edge|out.contains(&edge.parent))||[&t.rules.read,&t.rules.insert,&t.rules.update,&t.rules.delete].iter().any(|r|kernel::dependencies(r).iter().any(|t|out.contains(t))){out.insert(name.clone());}}
        if out.len()==before{return out;}
    }
}
fn reserve_observers(out:&mut Campaign,calls:usize)->bool{
    if !out.reserve(calls){return false;}
    if out.history_observer_calls+calls>32768{out.unsupported.insert("history read observers capped at 32768 calls; remaining disclosure combinations not covered".into());false}else{out.history_observer_calls+=calls;true}
}
fn observe(bound:&Bound,e:&mut Engine,rows:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,initial:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,actors:&[String],steps:&[Step],out:&mut Campaign){
    if !reserve_observers(out,0)||out.history_observer_calls>=32768{return;}
    let queries=bound.programs.iter().filter(|bp|bp.program.kind==ProgramKind::Query&&!bp.program.native).filter(|bp|{
        let json=serde_json::to_string(&bp.program).unwrap();
        if ["NewId","Emit","JobAs","JobQuery"].iter().any(|tag|json.contains(tag)){
            out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: generated-id/opaque history observer needs disclosure provenance",bp.program.name));false
        }else{true}
    }).collect::<Vec<_>>();
    if queries.is_empty(){return;}
    for inv in bound.invariants.iter().filter(|i|i.target=="read"){
        let direct=inv.policy.as_ref().is_some_and(|p|kernel::implies(&super::super::columns::admission(&bound.schema.tables[&inv.table],inv.column.as_deref()),p)==Ok(true));
        let indirect=bound.schema.maintains.iter().any(|m|m.source==inv.table)||bound.schema.tables.values().any(|t|[&t.rules.read,&t.rules.insert,&t.rules.update,&t.rules.delete].iter().any(|r|kernel::dependencies(r).contains(&inv.table)));
        if direct&&!indirect{continue;}

        let observing=observer_tables(bound,&inv.table);
        for row in rows.get(&inv.table).into_iter().flatten(){'actors:for viewer in actors{
            if out.exhausted(){return;}
            if out.violations.iter().any(|v|v.invariant_site==inv.site.to_string()&&v.viewer==*viewer&&v.reachability==Reachability::AuthoredFixtureTrace&&trace_key(&v.history)==trace_key(steps)){continue;}
            if !reserve_observers(out,1){return;}
            match policy_in(bound,inv,rows,viewer,None,row,e){Ok(false)=>{},Ok(true)=>continue,Err(why)=>{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: {why}",inv.target_label()));continue;}}
            let Ok(key)=row.field("id")else{continue;};
            if inv.column.as_ref().and_then(|c|bound.schema.tables[&inv.table].written_by.get(c)).is_some_and(|gate|!gate.audience&&row.field(&gate.owner).ok()!=Some(&Value::Id(viewer.clone()))){continue 'actors;}

            // A typed authored row return is already a payload witness. It does
            // not require an invalid absent world that deletes referenced data.
            for bp in queries.iter().copied().filter(|p|typed_source(&p.result,&inv.table)){
                let cases=fixture_cases(&bp.program,rows,actors,out);
                if cases.len()>32{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: typed read-observer arguments capped at 32",bp.program.name));}
                for args in cases.into_iter().take(32){
                    if !reserve_observers(out,2){return;}
                    let args=match materialize_arguments(e,&bp.program.args,&args,viewer){Ok(a)=>a,Err(why)=>{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: typed history observer asset/input preparation: {why}",bp.program.name));continue;}};
                    out.attempted+=1;
                    if let Ok(q)=e.query(&bp.program.name,Context::new(viewer,args.clone()).at(NOW))&&!q.unavailable&&typed_disclosure(&bp.result,&q.data,&inv.table,key,inv.column.as_deref(),row){
                        out.attempted+=1;
                        if !matches!(e.query(&bp.program.name,Context::new(viewer,args.clone()).at(NOW)),Ok(again) if !again.unavailable&&again.data==q.data){
                            out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: typed read payload is not repeatable on the committed image",bp.program.name));continue;
                        }
                        let added=violation(out,inv,steps,viewer,row,None,initial,if inv.column.is_some(){"column read observation"}else{"read"});
                        if added&&let Some(v)=out.violations.last_mut(){v.operation=bp.program.name.clone();v.args=args;v.rule_path="authored query repeatably returned a compiler-typed row/column payload of the protected table while current policy is false".into();}
                        continue 'actors;
                    }
                }
            }
            if inv.column.as_deref().is_some_and(|c|c!="id"){continue 'actors;}
            if bound.schema.tables.values().any(|t|t.columns.values().any(|k|matches!(k.required(),C::Ref{table} if table==&inv.table))){out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: history paired absence needs a referentially valid closure",inv.table));continue;}
            let Ok(id)=key.as_str()else{continue;};
            if !reserve_observers(out,3){return;}
            let mut absent=match fork(bound,e,Vec::new()){Ok(e)=>e,Err(why)=>{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: absent world fork: {why}",inv.table));continue;}};
            if let Err(why)=absent.seed_delete_at(&inv.table,id,NOW){out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: absent world refused: {why}",inv.table));continue;}
            if let Err(why)=validate_fixture(bound,&mut absent){out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: absent world invalid: {why}",inv.table));continue;}
            let absent_rows=match readback(&absent){Ok(r)=>r,Err(why)=>{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: absent world readback: {why}",inv.table));continue;}};
            if policy_in(bound,inv,&absent_rows,viewer,None,row,&mut absent)!=Ok(false){continue;}
            for bp in queries.iter().copied().filter(|p|observing.iter().any(|t|super::super::audit::mentions(&serde_json::to_value(&p.program).unwrap(),t))){
                let p=&bp.program;
                let cases=fixture_cases(p,rows,actors,out);
                if cases.len()>32{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: read-observer arguments capped at 32; remaining combinations not covered",p.name));}
                for args in cases.into_iter().take(32){
                    if !reserve_observers(out,4){return;}
                    let raw_args=args;
                    let args=match materialize_arguments(e,&p.args,&raw_args,viewer){Ok(a)=>a,Err(why)=>{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: paired history observer asset/input preparation: {why}",p.name));continue;}};
                    if args!=raw_args{
                        if !reserve_observers(out,2){return;}
                        absent=match fork(bound,e,Vec::new()){Ok(e)=>e,Err(why)=>{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: paired history argument ledger fork: {why}",p.name));continue;}};
                        if let Err(why)=absent.seed_delete_at(&inv.table,id,NOW){out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: paired history argument ledger absence: {why}",p.name));continue;}
                        if let Err(why)=validate_fixture(bound,&mut absent){out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: ledger absence world invalid: {why}",p.name));continue;}
                        if readback(&absent)!=Ok(absent_rows.clone()){out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: paired argument ledger changes the absent base world",p.name));continue;}
                    }
                    out.attempted+=2;
                    let (Ok(a),Ok(b))=(paired::observe(e,p,viewer,args.clone()),paired::observe(&mut absent,p,viewer,args.clone()))else{continue;};
                    if a==b{continue;}
                    if !matches!((paired::observe(e,p,viewer,args.clone()),paired::observe(&mut absent,p,viewer,args.clone())),(Ok(aa),Ok(bb)) if a==aa&&b==bb){out.unsupported.insert(format!("{} / {}: paired history observation is unstable or unavailable on repeat",p.name,inv.target_label()));continue;}
                    let added=violation(out,inv,steps,viewer,row,None,initial,"read");
                    if added&&let Some(v)=out.violations.last_mut(){v.operation=p.name.clone();v.args=args;v.observations=Some(paired::Observations{present:a,absent:b,absent_rows:absent_rows.clone()});}
                    continue 'actors;
                }
            }
        }}
    }
}
fn prior_policy(bound:&Bound,inv:&Invariant,rows:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,viewer:&str,old:Option<&Value>,row:&Value,prior:&mut Option<Engine>)->Result<bool,String>{
    match prior.as_mut(){Some(e)=>policy_in(bound,inv,rows,viewer,old,row,e),None=>policy(bound,inv,rows,viewer,old,row)}
        if candidates.len()>=LIMIT/4{break;}
    }
    'candidates:for n in 0..groups.iter().map(Vec::len).max().unwrap_or(0){for g in &groups{if let Some(c)=g.get(n){if candidate_keys.insert(serde_json::to_string(&(&c.0.name,&c.1,&c.2)).unwrap()){candidates.push(c.clone());}if candidates.len()==LIMIT{break 'candidates;}}}}
    if !out.reserve(1){return;}
    let mut base=match fork(bound,fixture,probes.clone()){Ok(e)=>e,Err(e)=>{out.unsupported.insert(e);return;}};
    for inv in bound.invariants.iter().filter(|i|i.target=="always"){
        for row in initial.get(&inv.table).into_iter().flatten(){for viewer in &actors{
            if !out.reserve(1){return;}
            if policy_in(bound,inv,&initial,viewer,None,row,&mut base)!=Ok(false){continue;}
            let mut witness=Campaign::default();violation(&mut witness,inv,&[],viewer,row,None,&initial,"always");
            if let Some(mut v)=witness.violations.pop(){v.reachability=Reachability::AuthoredFixtureTrace;v.operation="$fixture.assertion".into();v.rule_path="initial trusted fixture assertion; no authored mutation".into();v.scope="definite violation in authored fixture; initialization trusted, no authored transition claimed".into();out.violations.push(v);}break;
        }}
    }
    observe(bound,&mut base,&initial,&initial,&actors,&[],out);
    // Spend half the pair budget on grant changes followed by an operation
    // as the affected principal; interleave first steps and successor names.
    let mut role_groups=Vec::new();
    for (i,(p,_,args)) in candidates.iter().enumerate().take(LIMIT){
        let json=serde_json::to_string(p).unwrap();
        let rosters=ir::write_tables(p).into_iter().filter_map(|(t,_)|{
            let roles=super::super::privilege::role_columns(bound,&t);
            roles.iter().any(|c|json.contains(&format!("\"{c}\":"))).then_some(t)
        }).collect::<BTreeSet<_>>();
        if rosters.is_empty(){continue;}
        let mut recipients=BTreeSet::new();
        for (name,kind) in &p.args {if let Some(value)=args.get(name){match kind {
            ir::ArgType::Principal=>{if let Value::Id(actor)=value{recipients.insert(actor.clone());}},
            ir::ArgType::Ref{table} if rosters.contains(table)=>{
                if let Some(row)=initial.get(table).into_iter().flatten().find(|r|r.field("id").ok()==Some(value)){
                    for (column,kind) in &bound.schema.tables[table].columns{if matches!(kind.required(),C::Principal)&&let Ok(Value::Id(actor))=row.field(column){recipients.insert(actor.clone());}}
                }
            },_=>{}
        }}}
        let mut seen=BTreeSet::new();
        let successors=candidates.iter().enumerate().filter_map(|(j,(next,actor,_))|(next.name!=p.name&&recipients.contains(actor)&&seen.insert((next.name.clone(),actor.clone()))).then_some((i,j))).collect::<Vec<_>>();
        if !successors.is_empty(){role_groups.push(successors);}
    }
    let mut role_pairs=Vec::new();
    'pairs:for n in 0..role_groups.iter().map(Vec::len).max().unwrap_or(0){for group in &role_groups{if let Some(pair)=group.get(n){role_pairs.push(*pair);if role_pairs.len()==LIMIT/2{break 'pairs;}}}}
    // Breadth first, then targeted and diagonal pairs: no first step owns the cap.
    let history_limit=if out.history_limit==0{LIMIT}else{out.history_limit.min(LIMIT)};
    let singles=candidates.len().min(history_limit);
    let mut setups=Vec::new();let mut role_changes=Vec::new();
    let mut setup_seen=BTreeSet::new();let mut role_seen=BTreeSet::new();let mut pair_plan=Vec::new();
    for n in 0..singles+history_limit {
        if !out.reserve(1){break;}
        if n==singles&&!candidates.is_empty(){
            let mut seen=BTreeSet::new();
            'setup:for &second in &role_changes{for &first in &setups{if seen.insert((first,second)){pair_plan.push((first,second));if pair_plan.len()==LIMIT/2{break 'setup;}}}}
            for &(first,second) in &role_pairs{if pair_plan.len()==LIMIT{break;}if seen.insert((first,second)){pair_plan.push((first,second));}}
            let mut k=0;
            while pair_plan.len()<LIMIT{pair_plan.push((k%candidates.len(),(k/candidates.len()+k+1)%candidates.len()));k+=1;}
        }
        let indices=if n<singles{vec![n]}else if candidates.is_empty(){break;}else{let (first,second)=pair_plan[n-singles];vec![first,second]};
        let mut engine=match fork(bound,fixture,probes.clone()){Ok(e)=>e,Err(e)=>{out.unsupported.insert(e);break;}};
        out.sequences+=1;let mut steps=Vec::new();
        for i in indices{
            if !out.reserve(1){break;}
            let (p,viewer,args)=&candidates[i];let before=match readback(&engine){Ok(r)=>r,Err(e)=>{out.unsupported.insert(e);break;}};
            let args=match materialize_arguments(&mut engine,&p.args,args,viewer){Ok(a)=>a,Err(why)=>{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: history asset arguments: {why}",p.name));break;}};
            let mut prior=if bound.invariants.iter().any(|i|i.target!="read"&&i.policy.as_ref().is_some_and(RuleExpr::has_reference)){if !out.reserve(1){break;}match fork(bound,&engine,Vec::new()){Ok(e)=>Some(e),Err(why)=>{out.unsupported.insert(why);None}}}else{None};
            out.attempted+=1;*out.operations.entry(p.name.clone()).or_default()+=1;
            match engine.mutate(&p.name,None,Context::new(viewer,args.clone()).at(NOW)){
                Ok(result)=>{
                    let sent=matches!(result.state,snapback4_core::WriteState::Sent{..});
                    steps.push(Step{operation:p.name.clone(),viewer:viewer.clone(),args:args.clone(),result:serde_json::to_value(&result.state).unwrap()});
                    let after=match readback(&engine){Ok(r)=>r,Err(e)=>{out.unsupported.insert(e);break;}};
                    if sent&&n<singles{
                        let signature=serde_json::to_string(&(&p.name,viewer,&candidates[i].2)).unwrap();
                        let new_media=bound.invariants.iter().filter(|inv|inv.target=="read"&&initial.get(&inv.table).is_none_or(Vec::is_empty)).any(|inv|{
                            let table=&bound.schema.tables[&inv.table];
                            after.get(&inv.table).into_iter().flatten().any(|row|table.columns.iter().any(|(column,kind)|kind.contains_asset()&&row.field(column).is_ok_and(|v|*v!=Value::Null&&!matches!(v,Value::Array(xs) if xs.is_empty()))))
                        });
                        if new_media&&setup_seen.insert(signature.clone()){setups.push(i);}
                        let changed_role=bound.schema.tables.iter().any(|(table,_)|{
                            let columns=super::super::privilege::role_columns(bound,table);
                            !columns.is_empty()&&before.get(table).into_iter().flatten().any(|old|after.get(table).into_iter().flatten().find(|r|r.field("id").ok()==old.field("id").ok()).is_some_and(|new|columns.iter().any(|c|old.field(c).ok()!=new.field(c).ok())))
                        });
                        if changed_role&&role_seen.insert(signature){role_changes.push(i);}
                    }
                    if sent{changed(bound,p,&before,&after,&initial,viewer,&steps,&mut prior,&mut engine,out);}else{out.refused+=1;}
                    if sent&&before!=after{observe(bound,&mut engine,&after,&initial,&actors,&steps,out);column_reads::after_history(bound,&engine,&initial,&steps,out);}
                },Err(e)=>{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: history engine error {e}",p.name));break;}
            }
        }
    }
    out.unsupported.insert("history breadth/pairs bounded to 512; unvisited and longer traces remain uncovered".into());
}

- Their failures are recorded too, and this design avoids each:
  - a keystroke-rate value thunk flooded the wire;
  - `touch()` per keystroke restarted a publish generation;
  - agents looked for `useSnapbackEphemeral` and did not find
    `useSnapbackPresence`;
  - a `ttl 5s` misread the 5 s bound.

## 2. The authoring surface

### 2.1 Declaration

`ephemeral <life>` is a line in a table body, beside `expire at`, `sync` and
`online only`. `<life>` uses the duration units that schedules already
accept (`500ms`, `4s`, `1m`). It is required and must be between 1 second
and 60 seconds inclusive. Outside that range the compiler refuses with
`E_QUARRY_EPHEMERAL`, and the message states the range.

The table keeps the ordinary body grammar: columns, `unique`, `by`, `cap`,
`immutable`, `read`/`insert`/`update`/`delete` rules and `after insert`. The
implicit `id` column and `byId` stay. `default now` columns are allowed.
Some clauses cannot appear in an ephemeral table, because each would make
durable state depend on memory. The compiler refuses them with
`E_QUARRY_EPHEMERAL`, and the message names the clause and the fix:

| Refused on an ephemeral table | Why | Fix the message gives |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `sync …`, `online only`, `sync public`, a horizon | The rows never reach a device | Delete the line; devices read ephemeral rows from the server |
| `expire at .col` | Its life is the declaration | Delete it; `ephemeral <life>` sets every row's deadline |
| `sequence by`, `through`, `words(..)` indexes, asset columns (`image`, `video`), `once` | Durable custody | Keep that data in a durable table |
| Being the source of a `maintain`, a Following view or a feed | Durable derived data | Count or rank durable rows |

Nothing may depend on ephemeral rows except a query's own reads. The
compiler refuses each of these with `E_QUARRY_EPHEMERAL`:

- **any** table's rule or `after insert` that probes an ephemeral table
  (`exists typing[..]`), durable or ephemeral. An ephemeral row's
  visibility then depends only on its own deadline and on durable rows,
  which is what lets deadline expiry stay silent (§3.3);
- a column of any table whose type is an ephemeral table (a reference);
- `sync to typing[..]`;
- a durable mutation, job, schedule or action that reads or writes an
  ephemeral table;
- a `held` query that reads an ephemeral table (held means answered by the
  device).

This follows LLP 3000.009 §7: "Ephemeral state never decides a durable
invariant."

### 2.2 Ephemeral mutations

The binder classifies each mutation by the tables it writes and reads,
using an **exhaustive effect visitor**. `ir::write_tables` is not one: it
misses writes inside expressions such as `Expr::Block` (reviewed probe). The
visitor walks statements, nested expressions, loop domains and inlined
helpers.

- **Durable:** writes no ephemeral table and reads none. Unchanged.
- **Ephemeral:** writes only ephemeral tables. It may read durable tables,
  for example with `require exists members[roomId, viewer] else NOT_MEMBER`,
  and it may use `insert`, `upsert`, `update`, `delete`, `if absent`,
  `require`, `let` and `return`. It may not `emit`, call a native job, use a
  sequence, or write a durable table.
- **Mixed:** writes both kinds. Refused with `E_QUARRY_EPHEMERAL`: "split
  this into two mutations; call the ephemeral one from the client after the
  durable one".

A runtime boundary backs the classification. While an ephemeral mutation
runs, the layered row store (§4.3) refuses any durable write with an
internal refusal and rolls back. A durable mutation's store has no catalog
entry for an ephemeral table, so it cannot write one either.

The binder also marks an ephemeral mutation **`renews`** only when it can
prove that running it twice in a row has the same effect and result as
running it once. The proof is conservative:

- the program makes exactly one write, an `upsert` or `update` of an
  ephemeral table;
- that write's key and every patch value are built only from arguments,
  `viewer`, literals and `now`, never from a read (for example, no
  `n: old.n + 1`);
- anything before the write is a `require` guard, or a `let` used only by
  guards; those guards may read durable tables only, since an ephemeral
  write may invalidate an ephemeral guard;
- the program returns nothing.

`setTyping` renews. None of these does: `stopTyping`, an insert of a
reaction burst, a counter or toggle, or any operation that returns a value.
(Revision 3: Astra's delta probe compiled an upsert keyed by `viewer` that
patched `n: old.n + 1`. It met revision 2's key-only criterion but was not a
renewal.) Only `renews` operations are coalesced or suppressed by the
client (§2.4). All others are sent once per call, in order.

The classification appears in three places:

- `check`'s program listing, as `ephemeral` or `ephemeral, renews`;
- `check --residency`, where each ephemeral table is listed as
  `ephemeral <life>: server memory, never on a device`;
- the generated operation (§2.4).

An ephemeral mutation's semantics:

- **Life.** Each insert or upsert-insert of an ephemeral row stamps the
  deadline `now + life`. Each update, and each upsert that patches an
  existing row, stamps it again, even with an empty patch. That renewal is
  what a heartbeat means. `$deadline` is stamped as its own assignment:
  `recompute_deadline` returns early without `through`, and `merge_patch`
  copies the old deadline. A delete removes the row.
- **The renewal law.** Any write that stamps `$deadline` must pass the
  table's **insert** rule on the new image, in addition to whatever rule
  the write already needs (update, or insert on the insert arm). Extending a
  row's life re-admits it. So a principal who loses the right to insert,
  for example a removed member, cannot keep a row alive past one life,
  whatever the update rule says. Refusal is the update's ordinary refusal
  (`E_RULE` / `NOT_FOUND` per the writes card's table).
- **Rules.** Read, insert, update and delete rules are checked exactly as
  for durable tables. Durable tables are read at the reader's snapshot.
  Ephemeral tables are read at the memory store's state under its lock
  (§3.4 fixes the order).
- **Failure.** The mutation applies completely or not at all. A drop guard
  undoes the journal on every exit that is not a completed commit, including
  refusals, store errors and panics.
- **Result.** The result is the program's return value. There is no `seq`,
  no replay and no id-based idempotence. The client-minted `id` is only a
  correlation token. The server mints any new row ids itself and ignores
  `newIds`.
- **Unique keys, caps and constraints** apply within the memory store. An
  expired row does not collide, as with `expire at`.

### 2.3 Queries over ephemeral tables

A query reads an ephemeral table like any other:

```quarry
query typingIn(roomId: rooms):
  return typing where .roomId = roomId and .principal != viewer by .principal { principal }
```

The query runs on the server. On a device, a read that touches an ephemeral
table is `unknown`, and the client's existing server fallback answers it.
Answers carry `nextExpiry`, the earliest deadline among the ephemeral rows
the answer read.

**An ephemeral read is always its own query.** Reading `typing` inside a
query makes the whole query server-answered: a table without `sync` gives a
server site (`bind/residency.rs:209`), and any server site makes the query
`runs: "server"` (`residency.rs:122`). Joining typing into the chats list
would take the list off the device, so M6's cold-offline chats list would
break, and every typing write would re-run every user's chats list on the
server. `check` therefore prints a note, `N_EPHEMERAL_SERVER`, when a query
that would otherwise run on the device runs on the server only because it
reads an ephemeral table. The note's rewrite is: split the ephemeral read
into its own query and render it next to the device-held one.

The `ephemeral` card teaches the chats-list hint as a separate query,
rendered beside the device-held list:

```quarry
query typingInMyRooms():
  return typing where exists members[.roomId, viewer] and .principal != viewer first 100 by .roomId { roomId, principal }

## 3. Semantics

### 3.1 Time

- Deadlines use the server clock (`Shared::now`). That is the development
  clock under `dev`, so `dev clock advance` expires ephemeral rows as it
  expires `expire at` rows.
- A row is absent at `now >= deadline`, through the same `expired` check the
  runner already applies to expiring tables. The engine gives ephemeral
  tables a hidden `$deadline` column and an `$expiry` index, like
  `through`'s hidden deadline (`schema/through.rs`).
- **`$deadline` never reaches the wire.**
  - `recorded.rs` `public_image` returns a row unchanged when the table has
    no recorded fields, no `through` and no timeline slots. Ordinary
    ephemeral tables have exactly that shape. That fast path must also
    check for engine fields.
  - `value/disclosed.rs` `Disclosed::visible` must hide `$deadline` as it
    hides recorded fields.
  - Wire tests cover unshaped point reads, scans, and rows returned by
    mutations.
- Generated row types omit engine fields (`emit.rs:24`).

### 3.2 Authorization and revocation

- Ephemeral rows are read under their table's read rule, like any row.
- Rules are evaluated at read time, and ephemeral rows are never cached on a
  device. So a principal who loses membership sees nothing on their next
  read of that room.
- A durable commit to a table a watched query probes invalidates that
  query, as today.
- By the renewal law (§2.2), a departed member's rows outlive the departure
  by at most one life, whatever the update rule says.
- **Disclosure.** The `/changes` answer names ephemeral tables that were
  written. That is the same class as today's table-granular durable
  invalidation, which names every table a commit touched (`serve_changes.rs:80-94`).
  It adds no new leak class. Capacity refusals depend only on the writer's
  own rows (§3.5), except for the global backstop, which reveals only a
  server-wide aggregate, like HTTP admission.

### 3.3 Change notification

**Server state.**
- The memory store keeps:
  - an **epoch**, a `u64` that each committed ephemeral mutation
    increments;
  - an **incarnation**, a random id that changes whenever the store is
    cleared (§3.4);
  - a bounded ring of `(epoch, tables written)`.
- Deadline expiry does not bump the epoch.
  - For a **complete** answer this is safe. No rule probes an ephemeral table
    (§2.1), so expiry can change the answer only by removing rows the answer
    read. Those rows' deadlines are in its `nextExpiry`, and clients
    re-fetch at it.
  - An **incomplete** answer (`complete: false`, for example a scan cut by
    the authorization walk allowance behind hidden rows) can become more
    complete when hidden rows expire, and their deadlines cannot be
    exported. So a watched query with `ephemeralReads` whose latest answer
    is incomplete is re-fetched on a fixed, content-independent 1 s cadence
    while it stays incomplete. (Revision 3: Astra's delta probe put 8,192
    hidden rows ahead of a live row.)
  - The sweep only reclaims memory.

**Wake state, and no lost wakeups.**
- Polls get their own wake state: `Shared.wake: Mutex<Wake { seq, store_identity, generation, eph_incarnation, eph_epoch, ring_tail }>`,
  with a `polls: Condvar`.
- A durable commit updates `seq` under that mutex and notifies `polls`. It
  also notifies `committed` as today, for maintenance.
- An ephemeral commit publishes `(incarnation, epoch, ring tail)` into the
  wake state **while still holding the hub**, taking the wake mutex last
  (hub, then wake), and notifies `polls` only. `clear_and_rebind` publishes
  its new incarnation and hub binding (store identity, generation) together
  under that mutex. Wake publication therefore follows
  commit order. (Revision 3: publishing after releasing the hub let a paused
  commit 1 overwrite commit 2's wake. Astra's protocol model reproduced it.)
- A poll reads its wake snapshot under the wake mutex before `wait_timeout`,
  and re-reads it under the same mutex after a wake.
- On a wake where `seq` is unchanged, a poll takes no engine lock. Only a
  moved `seq` takes the engine for the store check, as today.
- Lock order is: engine mutex, then hub, then wake mutex. Nothing takes the
  hub or the engine while holding the wake mutex. Nothing takes the engine
  while holding the hub.

**`GET /changes` parameters.**
- `eph=<epoch>`.
- `ephi=<incarnation>`.
- `ephTables=<comma-separated table names>`:
  - absent means "wake me for every ephemeral commit";
  - present, possibly empty, means "wake me only for these tables".
- Clients send all three when the backend declares an ephemeral table.
  A client with no ephemeral interest sends `ephTables=` (empty). A
  durable-only backend keeps the old wire: clients omit all three, with no
  initial incarnation reset (§3.6).
- An online client without schema facts learns support from
  `X-Snapback-Ephemeral: 1` on ordinary responses/heartbeats, present only for
  ephemeral backends and exposed through CORS. It loads `/schema` and reissues
  any legacy discovery poll immediately. Generated read facts can enable the
  parameters before discovery. This preserves the entire durable-only request
  flow, including clients whose transport has no `/schema` endpoint.

**When the poll answers.** Exactly as today, plus two conditions:
- the store's sequence moved past `since`;
- the store identity changed;
- the generation differs from the generation at poll entry;
- the wait expired;
- a table in its interest was written after `eph` (new);
- the incarnation differs from `ephi` (new).

**Wake filtering and pacing.**
- A wake caused only by an ephemeral write outside the interest keeps
  waiting. It neither answers nor restarts the heartbeat.
- If the ring no longer covers `eph`, the answer names every table in the
  interest.
- An answer caused only by ephemeral writes is not sent sooner than 100 ms
  after the poll began. That bounds each client to 10 ephemeral-only
  re-polls a second, however fast a session writes.

**What the answer carries.**
- It gains `"ephemeral": { "epoch": n, "incarnation": "…", "tables": [...] }`.
- A poll with none of the three parameters answers exactly as before (the
  field omitted), apart from waking for ephemeral commits. A schema with no
  ephemeral table has none, so for it this is byte-identical to today.

**Re-issuing a poll.**
- Backend adoption reconciles every mounted watch against the adopted
  operation facts, including additions and removals. The client re-issues its
  held poll whenever its ephemeral interest set grows (§2.4).
- The re-issued poll's `eph` is the smallest epoch among the current
  incarnation's latest answers of its ephemeral watches.
- A watch with no answer yet contributes the client's last `/changes`
  epoch.
- A write that lands between an answer and the new poll is therefore always
  after `eph`, and the new poll answers at once.

**Query answers.**
- A query answer that read an ephemeral table carries
  `"ephemeral": { "epoch": n, "incarnation": "…" }`.
- The epoch is read under the hub lock, in the same critical section as the
  memory rows.

**Fencing.**
- The client keeps an **ephemeral revision**, a counter it tags onto every
  ephemeral query request and `/changes` poll it sends.
- Accepting a new incarnation advances the revision. The client then
  invalidates its outstanding ephemeral queries and its poll, and re-issues
  them.
- A response tagged with an older revision is dropped before its
  incarnation is looked at. So `A:50 → B:1 → delayed A:50` cannot reinstate
  the retired incarnation A. (Revision 3: under revision 2's rule, a
  different incarnation was always accepted. Astra's protocol model
  reproduced the resurrection.)
- Within the current revision, each watch remembers its own
  `(incarnation, epoch)` floor.
- An answer from a different incarnation is accepted, advances the
  revision, and replaces the floor.
- An answer from the same incarnation is accepted only if its epoch is at
  least the floor.
- This is independent of durable `seq` fencing (`minimumReadSeq`), and the
  two are never folded together.
- It applies in the online tier's `currentRead`, in `local-query.ts`'s
  publication path, and to `/changes` answers before they are judged stale.

**Presence re-polls.** Poll entry today runs `presence_reconnected`, which
opens an IMMEDIATE transaction (`engine/effects.rs:287-291`). With this
change, it first checks without a write transaction whether the principal
has a pending presence deferral. It opens the write transaction only if it
has one. The effect semantics are unchanged; ephemeral re-polls stop paying
a writer transaction each.
 ```
